PROBLEM
------------------------------
You are an assistant that helps in solving assigned Sokoban games.
Your task is to examine the provided Sokoban problem and find a solution.
All provided Sokoban problems are assigned in form of ASCII maps.
The mapping is the following:
'''
@ - Player
+ - Player on Goal
$ - Box
* - Box on Goal
. - Goal ( Empty )
# - Wall Brick
'''
The game is solved when the box is pushed into the goal position, hence when the position of '$' coincides with the position of '.'.
The player can move in all the empty spaces of the ASCII map while respecting the walls.
When the player is adjacent to a box, the player can push the box into an adjacent empty space.
After pushing a box, the new position of the agent will be the position of the box before the push.
The player cannot pull the box, only push it.
The actions you can perform in the game are:
'''
L - Move Left
R - Move Right
U - Move Up
D - Move Down
'''
All provided problems CAN be solved.
You must give your solution in form of a sequence of allowed actions, separated by commas.
You must give only the sequence of actions, without any additional text or explanation.
You must enclose your solution inside the tags <plan> </plan>.
The following is an example of a Sokoban problem and its solution:
Problem:
#####
#@ ##
## $ ##
# #
##. ##
## #
###
Solution:
<plan>
R ,R ,D ,D
</plan>

Here is the Sokoban problem to solve, enclosed in triple backticks:
'''
###
#.#
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
#$#
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
#@#
###

'''
================================================================================


ENVIRONMENT
------------------------------
You are an assistant that helps in solving assigned Sokoban games.
Your task is to examine the provided Sokoban problem and find a solution.
All provided Sokoban problems are assigned in form of ASCII maps.
The mapping is the following:
'''
@ - Player
+ - Player on Goal
$ - Box
* - Box on Goal
. - Goal ( Empty )
# - Wall Brick
'''
The game is solved when the box is pushed into the goal position, hence when the position of '$' coincides with the position of '.'.
The player can move in all the empty spaces of the ASCII map while respecting the walls.
When the player is adjacent to a box, the player can push the box into an adjacent empty space.
After pushing a box, the new position of the agent will be the position of the box before the push.
The player cannot pull the box, only push it.
The actions you can perform in the game are:
'''
L - Move Left
R - Move Right
U - Move Up
D - Move Down
'''
All provided problems CAN be solved.
You must give your solution in form of a sequence of allowed actions, separated by commas.
You must give only the sequence of actions, without any additional text or explanation.
You must enclose your solution inside the tags <plan> </plan>.
The following is an example of a Sokoban problem and its solution:
Problem:
#####
#@ ##
## $ ##
# #
##. ##
## #
###
Solution:
<plan>
R ,R ,D ,D
</plan>

Here is the Sokoban problem to solve, enclosed in triple backticks:
'''
###
#.#
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
#$#
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #
# #

================================================================================


FINAL-PLAN
------------------------------
<domain>
(define (domain sokoban_domain)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types
    player
    box
    goal
    location
    direction
  )
  (:predicates
    (player_at ?l - location)
    (box_at ?l - location)
    (goal_at ?l - location)
    (wall_at ?l - location)
    (connected ?l1 ?l2 - location ?d - direction)
  )

  (:action move_player_up
    :parameters (?from ?to - location)
    :precondition (and
      (player_at ?from)
      (connected ?from ?to up)
      (not (wall_at ?to))
      (not (box_at ?to))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (player_at ?from))
      (player_at ?to)
    )
  )

  (:action move_player_down
    :parameters (?from ?to - location)
    :precondition (and
      (player_at ?from)
      (connected ?from ?to down)
      (not (wall_at ?to))
      (not (box_at ?to))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (player_at ?from))
      (player_at ?to)
    )
  )

  (:action move_player_left
    :parameters (?from ?to - location)
    :precondition (and
      (player_at ?from)
      (connected ?from ?to left)
      (not (wall_at ?to))
      (not (box_at ?to))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (player_at ?from))
      (player_at ?to)
    )
  )

  (:action move_player_right
    :parameters (?from ?to - location)
    :precondition (and
      (player_at ?from)
      (connected ?from ?to right)
      (not (wall_at ?to))
      (not (box_at ?to))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (player_at ?from))
      (player_at ?to)
    )
  )

  (:action push_box_up
    :parameters (?player_from ?box_from ?box_to - location)
    :precondition (and
      (player_at ?player_from)
      (box_at ?box_from)
      (connected ?player_from ?box_from up) ; Player is immediately below the box
      (connected ?box_from ?box_to up)      ; Box can move upwards to an empty spot
      (not (wall_at ?box_to))
      (not (box_at ?box_to)) ; Target must not be occupied by another box (always true for a single box)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (player_at ?player_from))
      (player_at ?box_from) ; Player moves to the box's previous location
      (not (box_at ?box_from))
      (box_at ?box_to)
    )
  )

  (:action push_box_down
    :parameters (?player_from ?box_from ?box_to - location)
    :precondition (and
      (player_at ?player_from)
      (box_at ?box_from)
      (connected ?player_from ?box_from down) ; Player is immediately above the box
      (connected ?box_from ?box_to down)      ; Box can move downwards to an empty spot
      (not (wall_at ?box_to))
      (not (box_at ?box_to))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (player_at ?player_from))
      (player_at ?box_from)
      (not (box_at ?box_from))
      (box_at ?box_to)
    )
  )

  (:action push_box_left
    :parameters (?player_from ?box_from ?box_to - location)
    :precondition (and
      (player_at ?player_from)
      (box_at ?box_from)
      (connected ?player_from ?box_from left) ; Player is immediately to the right of the box
      (connected ?box_from ?box_to left)       ; Box can move leftwards to an empty spot
      (not (wall_at ?box_to))
      (not (box_at ?box_to))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (player_at ?player_from))
      (player_at ?box_from)
      (not (box_at ?box_from))
      (box_at ?box_to)
    )
  )

  (:action push_box_right
    :parameters (?player_from ?box_from ?box_to - location)
    :precondition (and
      (player_at ?player_from)
      (box_at ?box_from)
      (connected ?player_from ?box_from right) ; Player is immediately to the left of the box
      (connected ?box_from ?box_to right)      ; Box can move rightwards to an empty spot
      (not (wall_at ?box_to))
      (not (box_at ?box_to))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (player_at ?player_from))
      (player_at ?box_from)
      (not (box_at ?box_from))
      (box_at ?box_to)
    )
  )
)
</domain>
<problem>

  (:init

================================================================================


DOMAIN
------------------------------
(define (domain sokoban_domain)
  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)
  (:types
    player
    box
    goal
    location
    direction
  )
  (:predicates
    (player_at ?l - location)
    (box_at ?l - location)
    (goal_at ?l - location)
    (wall_at ?l - location)
    (connected ?l1 ?l2 - location ?d - direction)
  )

  (:action move_player_up
    :parameters (?from ?to - location)
    :precondition (and
      (player_at ?from)
      (connected ?from ?to up)
      (not (wall_at ?to))
      (not (box_at ?to))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (player_at ?from))
      (player_at ?to)
    )
  )

  (:action move_player_down
    :parameters (?from ?to - location)
    :precondition (and
      (player_at ?from)
      (connected ?from ?to down)
      (not (wall_at ?to))
      (not (box_at ?to))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (player_at ?from))
      (player_at ?to)
    )
  )

  (:action move_player_left
    :parameters (?from ?to - location)
    :precondition (and
      (player_at ?from)
      (connected ?from ?to left)
      (not (wall_at ?to))
      (not (box_at ?to))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (player_at ?from))
      (player_at ?to)
    )
  )

  (:action move_player_right
    :parameters (?from ?to - location)
    :precondition (and
      (player_at ?from)
      (connected ?from ?to right)
      (not (wall_at ?to))
      (not (box_at ?to))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (player_at ?from))
      (player_at ?to)
    )
  )

  (:action push_box_up
    :parameters (?player_from ?box_from ?box_to - location)
    :precondition (and
      (player_at ?player_from)
      (box_at ?box_from)
      (connected ?player_from ?box_from up) ; Player is immediately below the box
      (connected ?box_from ?box_to up)      ; Box can move upwards to an empty spot
      (not (wall_at ?box_to))
      (not (box_at ?box_to)) ; Target must not be occupied by another box (always true for a single box)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (player_at ?player_from))
      (player_at ?box_from) ; Player moves to the box's previous location
      (not (box_at ?box_from))
      (box_at ?box_to)
    )
  )

  (:action push_box_down
    :parameters (?player_from ?box_from ?box_to - location)
    :precondition (and
      (player_at ?player_from)
      (box_at ?box_from)
      (connected ?player_from ?box_from down) ; Player is immediately above the box
      (connected ?box_from ?box_to down)      ; Box can move downwards to an empty spot
      (not (wall_at ?box_to))
      (not (box_at ?box_to))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (player_at ?player_from))
      (player_at ?box_from)
      (not (box_at ?box_from))
      (box_at ?box_to)
    )
  )

  (:action push_box_left
    :parameters (?player_from ?box_from ?box_to - location)
    :precondition (and
      (player_at ?player_from)
      (box_at ?box_from)
      (connected ?player_from ?box_from left) ; Player is immediately to the right of the box
      (connected ?box_from ?box_to left)       ; Box can move leftwards to an empty spot
      (not (wall_at ?box_to))
      (not (box_at ?box_to))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (player_at ?player_from))
      (player_at ?box_from)
      (not (box_at ?box_from))
      (box_at ?box_to)
    )
  )

  (:action push_box_right
    :parameters (?player_from ?box_from ?box_to - location)
    :precondition (and
      (player_at ?player_from)
      (box_at ?box_from)
      (connected ?player_from ?box_from right) ; Player is immediately to the left of the box
      (connected ?box_from ?box_to right)      ; Box can move rightwards to an empty spot
      (not (wall_at ?box_to))
      (not (box_at ?box_to))
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (player_at ?player_from))
      (player_at ?box_from)
      (not (box_at ?box_from))
      (box_at ?box_to)
    )
  )
)
================================================================================

------------------------------

PROBLEM
------------------------------
You are an assistant that helps in solving assigned Sokoban games.
Your task is to examine the provided Sokoban problem and find a solution.
All provided Sokoban problems are assigned in form of ASCII maps.
The mapping is the following:
'''
@ - Player
+ - Player on Goal
$ - Box
* - Box on Goal
. - Goal ( Empty )
# - Wall Brick
'''
The game is solved when the box is pushed into the goal position, hence when the position of '$' coincides with the position of '.'.
The player can move in all the empty spaces of the ASCII map while respecting the walls.
When the player is adjacent to a box, the player can push the box into an adjacent empty space.
After pushing a box, the new position of the agent will be the position of the box before the push.
The player cannot pull the box, only push it.
The actions you can perform in the game are:
'''
L - Move Left
R - Move Right
U - Move Up
D - Move Down
'''
All provided problems CAN be solved.
You must give your solution in form of a sequence of allowed actions, separated by commas.
You must give only the sequence of actions, without any additional text or explanation.
You must enclose your solution inside the tags <plan> </plan>.

Problem:
#####
#@ ##
## $ ##
# #
##. ##
## #

<plan>

                "You are an assistant that helps in solving assigned Sokoban games.\nYour task is to examine the provided Sokoban problem and find a solution.\nAll provided Sokoban problems are assigned in form of ASCII maps.\nThe mapping is the following:\n'''\n@ - Player\n+ - Player on Goal\n$ - Box\n* - Box on Goal\n. - Goal ( Empty )\n# - Wall Brick\n'''\nThe game is solved when the box is pushed into the goal position, hence when the position of '$' coincides with the position of '.'.\nThe player can move in all the empty spaces of the ASCII map while respecting the walls.\nWhen the player is adjacent to a box, the player can push the box into an adjacent empty space.\nAfter pushing a box, the new position of the agent will be the position of the box before the push.\nThe player cannot pull the box, only push it.\nThe actions you can perform in the game are:\n'''\nL - Move Left\nR - Move Right\nU - Move Up\nD - Move Down\n'''\nAll provided problems CAN be solved.\nYou must give your solution in form of a sequence of allowed actions, separated by commas.\nYou must give only the sequence of actions, without any additional text or explanation.\nYou must enclose your solution inside the tags <plan> </plan>.\nThe following is an example of a Sokoban problem and its solution:\nProblem:\n#####\n#@ ##\n## $ ##\n# #\n##. ##\n## #\n###\nSolution:\n<plan>\nR ,R ,D ,D\n</plan>\n\nHere is the Sokoban problem to solve, enclosed in triple backticks:\n'''\n###\n#@#\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n#$#\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n# #\n#.#\n###\n\n'''",

    "pddl_domain": "(define (domain sokoban_domain)\n  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions :action-costs)\n  (:types\n    cell - object\n    player - object\n    box - object\n  )\n  (:predicates\n    (at_player ?p - player ?l - cell)\n    (at_box ?b - box ?l - cell)\n    (is_goal ?l - cell)\n    (is_wall ?l - cell)\n    (clear ?l - cell)\n    (adjacent ?l1 - cell ?l2 - cell)\n  )\n  (:functions (total-cost))\n\n  ;; Player moves to an empty adjacent cell\n  (:action move_player\n    :parameters (?p - player ?from - cell ?to - cell)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at_player ?p ?from)\n      (adjacent ?from ?to)\n      (clear ?to) ; Target cell must be clear (implies not a wall, not occupied by box/player)\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (at_player ?p ?from))\n      (at_player ?p ?to)\n      (clear ?from) ; Old player spot becomes clear\n      (not (clear ?to)) ; New player spot is no longer clear\n      (increase (total-cost) 1)\n    )\n  )\n\n  ;; Player pushes a box\n  (:action push_box\n    :parameters (?p - player ?b - box ?player_from - cell ?box_from - cell ?box_to - cell)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at_player ?p ?player_from)\n      (at_box ?b ?box_from)\n      (adjacent ?player_from ?box_from) ; Player next to box\n      (adjacent ?box_from ?box_to)     ; Box can be pushed to ?box_to\n      (clear ?box_to)                 ; Target cell for box must be clear\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (at_player ?p ?player_from))\n      (at_player ?p ?box_from) ; Player moves to box's old spot\n\n      (not (at_box ?b ?box_from))\n      (at_box ?b ?box_to) ; Box moves to new spot\n\n      (clear ?player_from) ; Player's original spot becomes clear\n      (not (clear ?box_from)) ; Box's original spot (now player's spot) is not clear\n      (not (clear ?box_to)) ; Box's new spot is not clear\n      (increase (total-cost) 1)\n    )\n  )\n)",

PROBLEM

You are an assistant that helps in solving assigned Sokoban games.
Your task is to examine the provided Sokoban problem and find a solution.
All provided Sokoban problems are assigned in form of ASCII maps.
The mapping is the following:
'''
@ - Player
+ - Player on Goal
$ - Box
* - Box on Goal
. - Goal ( Empty )
# - Wall Brick
'''
The game is solved when the box is pushed into the goal position, hence when the position of '$' coincides with the position of '.'.
The player can move in all the empty spaces of the ASCII map while respecting the walls.
When the player is adjacent to a box, the player can push the box into an adjacent empty space.
After pushing a box, the new position of the agent will be the position of the box before the push.
The player cannot pull the box, only push it.
The actions you can perform in the game are:
'''
L - Move Left
R - Move Right
U - Move Up
D - Move Down
'''
All provided problems CAN be solved.
You must give your solution in form of a sequence of allowed actions, separated by commas.
You must give only the sequence of actions, without any additional text or explanation.
You must enclose your solution inside the tags <plan> </plan>.
The following is an example of a Sokoban problem and its solution:
Problem:
#####
#@ ##
## $ ##
# #
##. ##
## #
###

<plan>
R ,R ,D ,D
</plan>

Box pushing actions\n    (:action push_box_up\n        :parameters (?p - agent ?b - sokobox ?p_from - location ?box_from - location ?box_to - location)\n        :precondition (and\n            (at_player ?p ?p_from)\n            (at_box ?b ?box_from)\n            (north ?p_from ?box_from) ; Player is south of the box\n            (north ?box_from ?box_to) ; Box moves north\n            (not (is_wall ?box_to))\n            (not (at_box box1 ?box_to))\n        )\n        :effect (and\n            (not (at_player ?p ?p_from))\n            (at_player ?p ?box_from)\n            (not (at_box ?b ?box_from))\n            (at_box ?b ?box_to)\n        )\n    )\n\n    (:action push_box_down\n        :parameters (?p - agent ?b - sokobox ?p_from - location ?box_from - location ?box_to - location)\n        :precondition (and\n            (at_player ?p ?p_from)\n            (at_box ?b ?box_from)\n            (south ?p_from ?box_from) ; Player is north of the box\n            (south ?box_from ?box_to) ; Box moves south\n            (not (is_wall ?box_to))\n            (not (at_box box1 ?box_to))\n        )\n        :effect (and\n            (not (at_player ?p ?p_from))\n            (at_player ?p ?box_from)\n            (not (at_box ?b ?box_from))\n            (at_box ?b ?box_to)\n        )\n    )\n\n    (:action push_box_left\n        :parameters (?p - agent ?b - sokobox ?p_from - location ?box_from - location ?box_to - location)\n        :precondition (and\n            (at_player ?p ?p_from)\n            (at_box ?b ?box_from)\n            (west ?p_from ?box_from) ; Player is east of the box\n            (west ?box_from ?box_to) ; Box moves west\n            (not (is_wall ?box_to))\n            (not (at_box box1 ?box_to))\n        )\n        :effect (and\n            (not (at_player ?p ?p_from))\n            (at_player ?p ?box_from)\n            (not (at_box ?b ?box_from))\n            (at_box ?b ?box_to)\n        )\n    )\n\n    (:action push_box_right\n        :parameters (?p - agent ?b - sokobox ?p_from - location ?box_from - location ?box_to - location)\n        :precondition (and\n            (at_player ?p ?p_from)\n            (at_box ?b ?box_from)\n            (east ?p_from ?box_from) ; Player is west of the box\n            (east ?box_from ?box_to) ; Box moves east\n            (not (is_wall ?box_to))\n            (not (at_box box1 ?box_to))\n        )\n        :effect (and\n            (not (at_player ?p ?p_from))\n            (at_player ?p ?box_from)\n            (not (at_box ?b ?box_from))\n            (at_box ?b ?box_to)\n        )\n    )\n)",

Player movement actions\n    (:action move_player_up\n        :parameters (?p - agent ?from - location ?to - location)\n        :precondition (and\n            (at_player ?p ?from)\n            (north ?from ?to)\n            (not (is_wall ?to))\n            (not (at_box box1 ?to))\n        )\n        :effect (and\n            (not (at_player ?p ?from))\n            (at_player ?p ?to)\n        )\n    )\n\n    (:action move_player_down\n        :parameters (?p - agent ?from - location ?to - location)\n        :precondition (and\n            (at_player ?p ?from)\n            (south ?from ?to)\n            (not (is_wall ?to))\n            (not (at_box box1 ?to))\n        )\n        :effect (and\n            (not (at_player ?p ?from))\n            (at_player ?p ?to)\n        )\n    )\n\n    (:action move_player_left\n        :parameters (?p - agent ?from - location ?to - location)\n        :precondition (and\n            (at_player ?p ?from)\n            (west ?from ?to)\n            (not (is_wall ?to))\n            (not (at_box box1 ?to))\n        )\n        :effect (and\n            (not (at_player ?p ?from))\n            (at_player ?p ?to)\n        )\n    )\n\n    (:action move_player_right\n        :parameters (?p - agent ?from - location ?to - location)\n        :precondition (and\n            (at_player ?p ?from)\n            (east ?from ?to)\n            (not (is_wall ?to))\n            (not (at_box box1 ?to))\n        )\n        :effect (and\n            (not (at_player ?p ?from))\n            (at_player ?p ?to)\n        )\n    )\n\n    ;; Box pushing actions\n    (:action push_box_up\n        :parameters (?p - agent ?b - sokobox ?p_from - location ?box_from - location ?box_to - location)\n        :precondition (and\n            (at_player ?p ?p_from)\n            (at_box ?b ?box_from)\n            (north ?p_from ?box_from) ; Player is south of the box\n            (north ?box_from ?box_to) ; Box moves north\n            (not (is_wall ?box_to))\n            (not (at_box ?b ?box_to)) ; Ensure destination is not occupied by this box (or another)\n        )\n        :effect (and\n            (not (at_player ?p ?p_from))\n            (at_player ?p ?box_from)\n            (not (at_box ?b ?box_from))\n            (at_box ?b ?box_to)\n        )\n    )\n\n    (:action push_box_down\n        :parameters (?p - agent ?b - sokobox ?p_from - location ?box_from - location ?box_to - location)\n        :precondition (and\n            (at_player ?p ?p_from)\n            (at_box ?b ?box_from)\n            (south ?p_from ?box_from) ; Player is north of the box\n            (south ?box_from ?box_to) ; Box moves south\n            (not (is_wall ?box_to))\n            (not (at_box ?b ?box_to))\n        )\n        :effect (and\n            (not (at_player ?p ?p_from))\n            (at_player ?p ?box_from)\n            (not (at_box ?b ?box_from))\n            (at_box ?b ?box_to)\n        )\n    )\n\n    (:action push_box_left\n        :parameters (?p - agent ?b - sokobox ?p_from - location ?box_from - location ?box_to - location)\n        :precondition (and\n            (at_player ?p ?p_from)\n            (at_box ?b ?box_from)\n            (west ?p_from ?box_from) ; Player is east of the box\n            (west ?box_from ?box_to) ; Box moves west\n            (not (is_wall ?box_to))\n            (not (at_box ?b ?box_to))\n        )\n        :effect (and\n            (not (at_player ?p ?p_from))\n            (at_player ?p ?box_from)\n            (not (at_box ?b ?box_from))\n            (at_box ?b ?box_to)\n        )\n    )\n\n    (:action push_box_right\n        :parameters (?p - agent ?b - sokobox ?p_from - location ?box_from - location ?box_to - location)\n        :precondition (and\n            (at_player ?p ?p_from)\n            (at_box ?b ?box_from)\n            (east ?p_from ?box_from) ; Player is west of the box\n            (east ?box_from ?box_to) ; Box moves east\n            (not (is_wall ?box_to))\n            (not (at_box ?b ?box_to))\n        )\n        :effect (and\n            (not (at_player ?p ?p_from))\n            (at_player ?p ?box_from)\n            (not (at_box ?b ?box_from))\n            (at_box ?b ?box_to)\n        )\n    )\n)",

PROBLEM
------------------------------
You are an assistant that helps in solving assigned Sokoban games.
Your task is to examine the provided Sokoban problem and find a solution.
All provided Sokoban problems are assigned in form of ASCII maps.
The mapping is the following:
'''
@ - Player
+ - Player on Goal
$ - Box
* - Box on Goal
. - Goal ( Empty )
# - Wall Brick
'''
The game is solved when the box is pushed into the goal position, hence when the position of '$' coincides with the position of '.'.
The player can move in all the empty spaces of the ASCII map while respecting the walls.
When the player is adjacent to a box, the player can push the box into an adjacent empty space.
After pushing a box, the new position of the agent will be the position of the box before the push.
The player cannot pull the box, only push it.
The actions you can perform in the game are:
'''
L - Move Left
R - Move Right
U - Move Up
D - Move Down
'''
All provided problems CAN be solved.
You must give your solution in form of a sequence of allowed actions, separated by commas.
You must give only the sequence of actions, without any additional text or explanation.
You must enclose your solution inside the tags <plan> </plan>.
The following is an example of a Sokoban problem and its solution:
Problem:
#####
#@ ##
## $ ##
# #
##. ##
## #
###
Solution:
<plan>
R ,R ,D ,D
</plan>

'''
###

PROBLEM
------------------------------
You are an assistant that helps in solving assigned Sokoban games.
Your task is to examine the provided Sokoban problem and find a solution.
All provided Sokoban problems are assigned in form of ASCII maps.
The mapping is the following:
'''
@ - Player
+ - Player on Goal
$ - Box
* - Box on Goal
. - Goal ( Empty )
# - Wall Brick
'''
The game is solved when the box is pushed into the goal position, hence when the position of '$' coincides with the position of '.'.
The player can move in all the empty spaces of the ASCII map while respecting the walls.
When the player is adjacent to a box, the player can push the box into an adjacent empty space.
After pushing a box, the new position of the agent will be the position of the box before the push.
The player cannot pull the box, only push it.
The actions you can perform in the game are:
'''
L - Move Left
R - Move Right
U - Move Up
D - Move Down
'''
All provided problems CAN be solved.
You must give your solution in form of a sequence of allowed actions, separated by commas.
You must give only the sequence of actions, without any additional text or explanation.
